Kids' Events Digest — Thu Oct 8 → Wed Oct 14, 2026 (ages under 7)

About this digest
  • Window: Thu Oct 8, 2026 through Wed Oct 14, 2026 — 7 days, America/Los_Angeles. Swept Wed Oct 7, 2026 (afternoon PT).
  • Home base: ZIP 94560 (Newark, CA). Distances: est. driving miles from 94560, taken from this run's sweep parts.
  • Coverage: baby/toddler/preschool storytimes (ages 0–6) plus all-ages kids/family events. 457 library events in the day lists below (Thu 10/8: 110 · Fri 10/9: 42 · Sat 10/10: 64 · Sun 10/11: 14 · Mon 10/12: 19 · Tue 10/13: 104 · Wed 10/14: 104) plus 112 Beyond listings (dated + standing, counted once — museum/bookstore cross-refs deduped); 569 total listings. School-age-only, teen, adult, reading-to-dog, and canceled events excluded. Closed branches excluded: Fremont Irvington (permanently closed), Contra Costa Kensington + Ygnacio Valley (long-term closed). Contra Costa Moraga is OPEN but on a storytime break through Oct 27 — only its all-month hidden-image search and the Tue 10/13 dated instance run.
  • Verification: [V] = re-pulled live this run (Wed 10/7). UNVERIFIED = pattern/carryover — not live-verified this run. No UNVERIFIED was upgraded to [V]. SFPL recurring storytimes are marked "(recurring, verify)" — from the Feb 2026 SFPL newsletter; verify at sfpl.org before driving. SFPL entries marked * need reservations. SJPL Sat 10/10 entries marked carried/UNVERIFIED in the list were verified Oct 3–5, not re-read Oct 7 (page-3 gap; 2 Sat items unidentified). Sunnyvale Oct 9–13 entries are snippet/aggregator-verified from the library feed.
  • Flags: "free ticket" = pick up at the Children's desk before the program (Hayward rule, repeated in every day's Notes and in Closures: free ticket 30 min before, every adult AND child, no advance reservations). ⚠️ = choking-hazard flags for a 2-year-old (LEGO/small-piece/bead crafts). "borderline" = skews school-age but all-ages. All-day events are in Activities with "all day" wording so the renderer buckets them separately.
  • All library events are free unless noted. Non-library sources (bookstores, parks, museums, zoos, nature, art studios, Eventbrite, parent calendars) are mostly PAID — costs shown distinctly (PAID/FREE) in Beyond.
  • Layout: each day is split into Storytimes vs Activities (nearest-first within each). A Nearest | Earliest | Latest toggle re-sorts every list; all-day events go in the All-day bucket.
  • Mon Oct 12 is Indigenous Peoples' Day: SMCL, Palo Alto, SCCLD, Berkeley and SSF libraries are CLOSED (SMCL/SSF also Sun 10/11; Hayward closed Sun–Mon); SJPL has zero in-scope events Mon. SMCL's Thu 10/1 Staff Development closure is OUTSIDE this window and is not presented as in-window. Children's Fairyland is closed Tue 10/13.
